public class CM extends java.lang.Object {

    static int compound() {
        // @method compound()I
        // @declaration a static method of `CM`, member flags 0x0008

        // @declaration a static method of `CM`, member flags 0x0008
        // recovered from bytecode; presentation is not claimed to compile
        int local0 = 5;
